<!-- EXAMPLE:U127 -->
The teacher used concrete objects to explain the plan's dimensions.
`教师用实物解释了方案的尺寸。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U127 -->
The teacher used concrete objects to teach addition and subtraction.
`教师用实物教加法和减法。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U128 -->
Brick is a concrete noun, whereas safety names an abstract idea.
`brick 是具体名词，而 safety 表示抽象概念。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U129 -->
As Mr Blunkett emphasised, nothing is yet set in concrete.
`正如布伦基特先生强调的，目前一切都还没有定死。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U130 -->
Minerals gradually concreted around the buried pipe.
`矿物质逐渐在埋设的管道周围凝结成块。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U131 -->
The drawings concreted the committee's vague idea into a workable design.
`这些图纸把委员会模糊的想法具体化为可实施的设计。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U132 -->
The proposal looked simple on paper but proved difficult in the concrete.
`提案在纸上看似简单，落实到实际中却很难。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U133 -->
She explained the repair costs concretely, item by item.
`她逐项具体说明了维修费用。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U133 -->
She gave figures to make her point more concretely.
`她列出数字，更具体地说明自己的观点。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U134 -->
The concreteness of the new timetable reassured residents.
`新时间表的具体明确让居民感到安心。`

<!-- WORD:W012 -->
# consult

<!-- EXAMPLE:U135 -->
Consult your doctor about how much exercise you should attempt.
`关于适合做多少运动，请咨询医生。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U135 -->
He needed to consult with an attorney.
`他需要咨询一名律师。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U135 -->
If you are in any doubt, consult a financial adviser.
`如有任何疑问，请咨询财务顾问。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U136 -->
The manager consulted the owner for approval before closing the hall.
`经理关闭礼堂前，向业主请求批准。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U137 -->
After consulting with her daughter and manager she decided to take on the part, on her terms.
`她与女儿和经纪人商量后，决定按自己的条件接下这个角色。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U137 -->
The two countries will have to consult their allies.
`两国将不得不与盟友磋商。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U137 -->
The umpires consulted quickly.
`裁判们迅速商量了一下。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U138 -->
Consult the chart on page 44 for the correct cooking times.
`请查阅第四十四页的表格，确认正确烹调时间。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U138 -->
Before setting off, she consulted her watch.
`出发前，她看了看手表上的时间。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U139 -->
The committee consulted residents' interests when redesigning the entrance.
`委员会重新设计入口时顾及了居民利益。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U140 -->
Shirley's brother is now a consultant heart surgeon in Sweden.
`雪莉的兄弟如今在瑞典担任心脏外科高级专科医生。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U141 -->
An engineering consultant examined the cracked foundation.
`一名工程顾问检查了开裂的地基。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U141 -->
A team of management consultants was sent in to reorganise the department.
`一个管理顾问团队受派来重组该部门。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U142 -->
Next week he'll be in Florida for consultations with the President.
`下周他将去佛罗里达与总统举行磋商。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U142 -->
The plans were drawn up in consultation with the World Health Organisation.
`这些计划是在与世界卫生组织磋商后拟定的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U143 -->
A personal diet plan is devised after a consultation with a nutritionist.
`私人饮食方案会在咨询营养师后拟定。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U144 -->
The hospital arranged a consultation among three surgeons before the operation.
`医院在手术前安排了三名外科医生会诊。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U145 -->
The studies available for consultation should help to avoid the pitfalls.
`可供查阅的研究资料应该有助于避免这些陷阱。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U146 -->
A consultative committee reviewed the proposed repairs.
`咨询委员会审查了拟议的修缮工程。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U146 -->
The consultative committee on local government finance proposed changes to the grant system.
`地方政府财政咨询委员会提出了调整拨款制度的建议。`

<!-- WORD:W013 -->
# contract

<!-- EXAMPLE:U147 -->
The company won a prestigious contract for work on Europe's tallest building.
`公司赢得了一份参与欧洲最高建筑施工的重要合同。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U147 -->
He was given a seven-year contract with an annual salary of $150,000.
`他获得了一份年薪十五万美元、为期七年的合同。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U148 -->
The solicitor placed the signed contract in the archive.
`律师把签好的合同文件放入档案。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U149 -->
For them, marriage was a contract that neither would enter lightly.
`对他们而言，婚姻是一份谁都不会轻率作出的有约束力的承诺。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U150 -->
The players made their contract by winning nine tricks.
`牌手赢了九墩，完成了定约。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U151 -->
The convictions resulted in the local crime bosses putting a contract on him.
`这些定罪使当地黑帮头目悬赏雇人杀他。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U152 -->
By now she was under contract to MGM and on her way to Hollywood.
`此时她已与米高梅签约，正前往好莱坞。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U153 -->
You can contract with us to deliver your cargo.
`你可以与我们签约，由我们运送货物。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U153 -->
The Boston Museum of Fine Arts has already contracted to lease part of its collection to a museum in Japan.
`波士顿美术博物馆已签约将部分藏品租借给日本的一家博物馆。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U153 -->
The museum contracted for specialist repairs to its roof.
`博物馆签订了由专业人员修缮屋顶的合同。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U154 -->
Firms can contract out work to one another.
`公司可以互相外包工作。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U154 -->
When Barclays Bank contracted out its cleaning, the new company was cheaper.
`巴克莱银行把清洁业务外包后，新公司收费更低。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U154 -->
The report described the trend of contracting services out rather than performing them in-house.
`报告描述了将服务外包而非由内部人员承担的趋势。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U154 -->
The council contracted the repairs out to a local firm.
`市议会把维修工程外包给当地一家公司。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U155 -->
Employees can contract out of their employer's occupational pension scheme.
`雇员可以选择退出雇主的职业养老金计划。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U155 -->
They signed a deal which converts into a pay-as-you-go service unless you contract out.
`他们签订了一项协议：除非你选择退出，否则服务将转为按使用量付费。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U156 -->
Staff may contract in to the optional savings scheme each April.
`员工每年四月可以签约加入自愿储蓄计划。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U157 -->
The council contracted the builder to complete the roof by June.
`市议会与承建商订约，要求其在六月前完工屋顶。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U158 -->
Blood is only expelled from the heart when it contracts.
`心脏收缩时血液才被泵出。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U158 -->
New research shows that an excess of meat and salt can contract muscles.
`新研究显示，过量的肉类和盐可能使肌肉收缩。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U158 -->
She contracted her brow as she studied the damaged beam.
`她检查受损的横梁时皱起了眉头。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U159 -->
The manufacturing economy contracted in October for the sixth consecutive month.
`制造业经济在十月连续第六个月收缩。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U160 -->
He contracted the disease from a blood transfusion.
`他因输血感染了这种疾病。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U160 -->
Ovarian cancer is the sixth most common cancer contracted by women.
`卵巢癌是女性罹患的第六常见癌症。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U161 -->
She contracted a formal marriage to a British ex-serviceman.
`她与一名前英国退役军人正式成婚。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U162 -->
The two restorers contracted a friendship during the long project.
`两位修复师在漫长的项目中建立了友谊。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U162 -->
He contracted a habit of checking every window before leaving.
`他养成了出门前检查每扇窗户的习惯。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U163 -->
The trust contracted a debt to keep the building safe through winter.
`信托机构为让建筑安全过冬而背上了一笔债务。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U164 -->
In informal speech, speakers often contract 'do not' to 'don't'.
`在非正式口语中，说话人常把 do not 缩略为 don't。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U165 -->
The exhibit compares contractive and expansive movements in the muscle model.
`展品比较了肌肉模型中的收缩与扩张运动。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U166 -->
The contractor inspected the ceiling before submitting a price.
`承包商在报价前检查了天花板。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U166 -->
The company is a major U.S. defense contractor.
`该公司是美国一家大型国防承包商。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U167 -->
The company has not fulfilled certain contractual obligations.
`该公司尚未履行某些合同义务。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U168 -->
Rank was contractually obliged to hand him a cheque for $30 million.
`依照合同，兰克必须交给他一张三千万美元的支票。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U169 -->
Foods and fluids are mixed in the stomach by its muscular contractions.
`食物和液体靠胃部肌肉的收缩混合在一起。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U169 -->
The chart shows the contraction and expansion of blood vessels.
`图表显示了血管的收缩与扩张。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U170 -->
Her contractions became more frequent as labour progressed.
`随着产程推进，她的宫缩越来越频繁。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U171 -->
In 'It's late,' 'it's' is a contraction of 'it is'.
`在 It's late 中，it's 是 it is 的缩略形式。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U172 -->
The report recorded a contraction in local manufacturing.
`报告记录了当地制造业规模的缩减。`

<!-- WORD:W014 -->
# corridor
<!-- EXAMPLE:U173 -->
A long corridor connected the reading rooms to the entrance.
`一条长走廊把阅览室与入口连了起来。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U173 -->
An open-air corridor joined the building's two wings.
`一条室外过道连接了建筑的两翼。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U174 -->
Passengers waited in the corridor outside the railway compartments.
`乘客在列车包厢外的通道里等候。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U175 -->
The republic lay in a narrow corridor of disputed land.
`这个共和国位于一条有争议的狭长地带。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U176 -->
Aircraft were instructed to remain within the designated air corridor.
`飞机被要求保持在指定空中走廊内。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U177 -->
New workshops grew along the busy transport corridor.
`繁忙交通走廊沿线出现了新的工坊。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U177 -->
The new bus service connects towns along the M4 corridor.
`新开通的公交线路连接了 M4 高速公路沿线的城镇。`

<!-- WORD:W015 -->
# damage
<!-- EXAMPLE:U178 -->
He maliciously damaged a car with a baseball bat.
`他恶意用棒球棒砸坏了一辆汽车。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U178 -->
The sun can damage your skin.
`阳光可能损伤皮肤。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U179 -->
Jackson doesn't want to damage his reputation as a political personality.
`杰克逊不想损害自己作为政治人物的声誉。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U180 -->
The blast caused extensive damage to the house.
`爆炸对房屋造成了大面积破坏。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U180 -->
Many professional boxers end their careers with brain damage.
`许多职业拳击手退役时已有脑损伤。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U181 -->
Incidents of this type cause irreparable damage to relations with the community.
`这类事件会对与社区的关系造成无法弥补的损害。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U181 -->
The closure did more than cause repair costs: the loss of the town's only meeting place was lasting damage.
`关闭场馆造成的不只是维修费用：镇上失去唯一的聚会场所，是一种持久的损失。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U182 -->
He was vindicated in court and damages were awarded.
`他在法庭上恢复了清白，并获得赔偿金。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U183 -->
After the repair estimate arrived, she asked, 'What's the damage?'
`维修报价送达后，她问：“要花多少钱？”`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U184 -->
Is the recycling process in itself damaging to the environment?
`回收过程本身会对环境造成损害吗？`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U184 -->
It was a most damaging admission.
`这是一项极其有损声誉的承认。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U185 -->
The false report damagingly distorted the residents' account.
`那份虚假报告以有害的方式歪曲了居民的陈述。`

<!-- WORD:W016 -->
# deadline
<!-- EXAMPLE:U186 -->
We were not able to meet the deadline because of manufacturing delays.
`由于生产延误，我们未能赶上截止日期。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U186 -->
The deadline for submissions to the competition will be Easter Sunday.
`该竞赛的投稿截止日是复活节星期日。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U187 -->
The prison map marked a deadline beyond which prisoners were forbidden to walk.
`监狱地图标出了囚犯不得跨越的警戒线。`

<!-- WORD:W017 -->
# demolish
<!-- EXAMPLE:U188 -->
A storm moved directly over the island, demolishing buildings and flooding streets.
`风暴径直掠过岛屿，摧毁建筑并淹没街道。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U189 -->
The impact demolished the fragile display case.
`撞击把脆弱的展柜彻底毁坏了。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U190 -->
The reform demolished the old system of closed appointments.
`改革推翻了旧的封闭任命制度。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U191 -->
Our intention was to demolish the rumours that have surrounded him.
`我们的目的是彻底驳倒围绕他的种种传闻。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U192 -->
After the long shift, the crew demolished a plate of sandwiches.
`长班结束后，施工队员们三两下就把一盘三明治吃光了。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U193 -->
The project required the total demolition of the old bridge.
`该项目需要彻底拆除旧桥。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U194 -->
The demolisher removed the unsafe wall before the rest of the site opened.
`拆除人员在工地其余部分开放前拆掉了危险墙体。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U195 -->
A demolitionist explained why the damaged annex could not be saved.
`一名拆除专业人员解释了为何受损附楼无法保留。`

<!-- WORD:W018 -->
# diagram
<!-- EXAMPLE:U196 -->
You can reduce long explanations to simple charts or diagrams.
`你可以把冗长的解释简化成图表或示意图。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U197 -->
The diagram shows each stage of the roof repair.
`示意图展示了屋顶维修的每个阶段。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U198 -->
The proof is easier to follow with a clear geometric diagram.
`有一幅清楚的几何图形，证明就更容易理解。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U199 -->
The engineer diagrammed the ventilation system for the residents.
`工程师为居民画出了通风系统示意图。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U200 -->
This is the virus in very crude simple diagrammatic form.
`这是一幅十分粗略、简单的病毒示意图。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U201 -->
The booklet presents the repair sequence diagrammatically.
`手册用图示方式呈现了维修顺序。`

<!-- WORD:W019 -->
# dome
<!-- EXAMPLE:U202 -->
The builders repaired the dome above the main hall.
`施工人员修复了主厅上方的穹顶。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U203 -->
The observatory dome rotated to reveal the night sky.
`天文台的圆顶转动后露出了夜空。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U204 -->
A dome of branches shaded the old path.
`树枝形成一片穹幕，遮住了旧路。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U205 -->
From the bridge, the dome of the hill looked almost perfectly round.
`从桥上看，那座山丘的圆拱状顶部几乎是正圆形的。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U477 -->
The new lamp has a frosted glass dome.
`新灯有一个磨砂玻璃制的穹顶状灯罩。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U206 -->
The geological map marks a dome beneath the valley.
`地质图标出了山谷下方的一处穹状构造。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U207 -->
In the old comic, the guard complains that he bumped his dome on a low beam.
`在那本旧漫画里，守卫抱怨自己的脑袋撞上了低矮横梁。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U208 -->
The poet called the rebuilt library a dome of learning.
`诗人把重建后的图书馆称作一座知识殿堂。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U209 -->
The builders domed the new gallery with timber ribs.
`建筑工人用木肋架给新展厅建起穹顶。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U210 -->
The great hall had a domed ceiling.
`大厅有一面穹形天花板。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U211 -->
A domelike canopy covered the model village.
`一座穹顶般的顶棚覆盖着模型村庄。`

<!-- WORD:W020 -->
# efficient
<!-- EXAMPLE:U212 -->
With today's more efficient contraception women can plan their families and careers.
`有了如今更有效的避孕措施，女性可以规划家庭和事业。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U213 -->
An efficient coordinator kept the repair schedule on track.
`一位能干的协调员让维修进度保持正轨。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U214 -->
There are many ways to increase agricultural efficiency in the poorer areas of the world.
`有许多办法能提高世界贫困地区的农业效率。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U214 -->
The clinic improved its energy efficiency by replacing the old heating system.
`诊所更换了旧供暖系统，提高了能源利用效率。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U215 -->
The engineer measured the motor's efficiency under full load.
`工程师测量了电机满载时的效率。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U216 -->
I work very efficiently and am decisive, and accurate in my judgement.
`我工作很高效，判断果断而准确。`

<!-- WORD:W021 -->
# estimate
<!-- EXAMPLE:U217 -->
Try to estimate how many steps it will take to get to a close object.
`试着估计走到近处一件物体要迈多少步。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U217 -->
I estimate that the total cost for treatment will be $12,500.
`我估计治疗总费用将是一万二千五百美元。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U217 -->
He estimated the speed of the winds from the degree of damage.
`他根据破坏程度估算风速。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U217 -->
Some analysts estimate its current popularity at around ten per cent.
`一些分析人士估计它目前的支持率约为百分之十。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U217 -->
We have too little information to estimate yet.
`我们掌握的信息还太少，暂时无法估算。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U218 -->
The committee estimated the building's cultural value from its history.
`委员会根据建筑的历史评估了其文化价值。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U219 -->
The official estimate of the election result was released this morning.
`选举结果的官方估计数字于今天上午公布。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U219 -->
This figure is five times the original estimate.
`这个数字是最初估算的五倍。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U220 -->
I hadn't been far wrong in my estimate of his grandson's capabilities.
`我对他孙子能力的判断并没有错得太远。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U221 -->
The builder sent a written estimate for the roof repairs.
`建筑商寄来了屋顶维修的书面报价。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U222 -->
There are an estimated 90,000 gangsters in the country.
`估计该国有九万名黑帮成员。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U223 -->
He has gone down considerably in my estimation.
`我对他的评价已经大幅降低了。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U223 -->
In my estimation, the route is still unsafe.
`在我看来，这条路线仍不安全。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U224 -->
The team's estimation of repair costs changed after the inspection.
`实地检查后，团队对维修费用的估算改变了。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U224 -->
The report included estimations of pre-tax profits of £12.25 million.
`报告列出了税前利润约为一千二百二十五万英镑的估算结果。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U225 -->
The old letter says the mason was held in high estimation by the town.
`那封旧信说镇民十分敬重那位石匠。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U226 -->
The report's estimative assessment of repair costs will be revised after the survey.
`报告中基于估计作出的维修费用评估将在勘查后修订。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U227 -->
The estimator measured the damaged roof before preparing a quote.
`估价员在准备报价前测量了受损屋顶。`

<!-- WORD:W022 -->
# facility
<!-- EXAMPLE:U228 -->
What recreational facilities are now available?
`现在有哪些娱乐设施可用？`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U228 -->
The problem lies in getting patients to a medical facility as soon as possible.
`问题在于要尽快把病人送到医疗机构。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U228 -->
The library provides facilities for independent study, including computers and quiet rooms.
`图书馆提供计算机和安静的房间等资源，方便读者自主学习。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U229 -->
It is very useful to have an overdraft facility.
`有透支额度很方便。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U230 -->
He and Marcia shared a facility for languages.
`他和玛西娅都很有语言天赋。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U231 -->
The old shutters opened with surprising facility after the repairs.
`维修之后，这些旧百叶窗打开时出奇顺畅。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U232 -->
The guide spoke with remarkable facility about the building's history.
`导览员介绍建筑历史时十分流畅娴熟。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U232 -->
Her facility of expression helped the group understand a difficult proposal.
`她流畅的表达帮助大家理解了一项复杂的提案。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U233 -->
The company opened a new production facility outside the city.
`公司在城外开设了一座新工厂。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U234 -->
The new airport will facilitate the development of tourism.
`新机场将促进旅游业发展。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U234 -->
He argued that the economic recovery had been facilitated by his tough stance.
`他认为强硬立场促进了经济复苏。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U235 -->
The facilitation of public meetings was part of the council's plan.

<!-- WORD:W031 -->
# permit
<!-- EXAMPLE:U323 -->

<!-- EXAMPLE:U323 -->

<!-- EXAMPLE:U325 -->

`道格拉斯一生中只有一次放任自己情绪失控。`

Permit me to give you some advice.
`请允许我给你一点建议。`
<!-- EXAMPLE:U326 -->

`在此工作的多数外国人持有工作许可证。`

`她在移居国外前获发了工作许可证。`

# propose

<!-- WORD:W038 -->
# safety

<!-- EXAMPLE:U421 -->

# sustainable

<!-- SENTENCE:S001 USE:U001,U002,U003 -->
At first, the flood-damaged archive had no public access: the riverside access was blocked, and residents had no access to its online records.

<!-- SENTENCE:S004 USE:U007 -->

<!-- SENTENCE:S005 USE:U008,U009,U012,U013 -->
The upper entrance remained accessible by foot, but the archive's digital catalogue was scarcely accessible to residents, so Mara put both physical accessibility and access to information on the agenda.
`上层入口仍可步行到达，但居民很难使用档案馆的电子目录，因此玛拉把场所可达性和信息获取都列入议程。`
<!-- SENTENCE:S006 USE:U010,U011,U014,U015 -->
Noor, an accessible curator, rewrote the technical summary in accessible language; visitors later praised the accessibility of her explanation because she presented the evidence accessibly.
`策展人努尔平易近人，她用通俗语言重写技术摘要；访客后来称赞她的讲解容易理解，因为她把证据说得明白易懂。`
<!-- SENTENCE:S007 USE:U016,U017,U020 -->
The riverside community had adapted to seasonal flooding, but adapting a damaged storehouse into a resilient public hall would demand a more radical adaptation of its rooms.
`河边社区早已适应季节性洪水，但要把受损的库房改成抗灾的公共大厅，还得对房间作更大幅度的改造。`
<!-- SENTENCE:S008 USE:U018,U019 -->

<!-- SENTENCE:S241 USE:U019 -->
Across the room, Mara's scale model showed another adaptation: the old storehouse refitted as a public hall.

<!-- SENTENCE:S009 USE:U021 -->
A natural history display showed the water bird's webbed feet as an adaptation to life along the river.
`自然史展览把水鸟的蹼足展示为其适应河边生活形成的特征。`

<!-- SENTENCE:S242 USE:U474 -->
A second panel traced the species' adaptation to seasonal floods over generations, while a darkened viewing alcove let visitors experience sensory adaptation as their eyes adjusted to the low light.
`第二块展板介绍了这一物种经过多代逐渐适应季节性洪水的过程；一处灯光昏暗的观看角落则让访客在眼睛逐渐适应弱光时体验感官适应。`
<!-- SENTENCE:S010 USE:U022,U023,U024,U025 -->
An adaptive flood alarm could adjust its warnings, the wide doorway was well adapted to wheelchair access, adaptable volunteers could learn new roles, and the old furniture was adaptable to different exhibitions.

<!-- SENTENCE:S011 USE:U026,U027,U028 -->
The team valued the volunteers' adaptability: they responded adaptably to each setback, and Noor called the storehouse conversion an adaption in her first plan.

<!-- SENTENCE:S014 USE:U036,U038 -->
